1c:["$","p",null,{"children":["A type name can be followed by type arguments in ",["$","code",null,{"children":"<>"}],": ",["$","code",null,{"children":"Map<string, uint32>"}],",\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"Stack<Stack<uint32>>"}],".
